# CareerForge — Backlog

**Status:** v1 ledger — 12-week roadmap complete · **Last updated:** 2026-07-26

Stories are grouped by milestone (M0–M4, matching [PLAN.md](./PLAN.md)) and ordered by priority within each. Sizes: **S** (≤ half day) · **M** (~1 day) · **L** (2–3 days). Statuses: `not started` → `in progress` → `done` (update on completion — CLAUDE.md rule). Definition of done for every story: code + tests + migration (if schema) + docs updated, and `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test` green.

---

## M0 — Foundation (Weeks 1–2)

### M0-01 · Privacy guardrails before first commit · **S** · `done` *(2026-07-12: pre-push verification passed — clean `git ls-files`, full-history gitleaks scan, no private paths in any commit; repo published to github.com/carlos-gutz-25/careerforge. CI profile-guard green on first push; the gitleaks job errors on the initial push only because the root commit has no parent for its diff range — valid on subsequent pushes.)*

> ⚠️ Must be completed before anything is committed to the public repo.

- **Given** a fresh clone, **then** `.gitignore` excludes `docs/profile/`, `.env`, and local data dumps, and `git status` shows none of them.
- `docs/profile.example/` exists with a fully fictional profile (resume, projects, links, job criteria) mirroring the real structure.
- gitleaks runs as a pre-commit hook and in CI; a seeded fake secret in a test branch is caught.
- README states the public-repo/private-data policy.

### M0-02 · pnpm workspace scaffold · **M** · `done` *(2026-07-12: 8 workspaces + shared config in packages/config; typecheck/lint/test green from root; boundary enforcement verified by negative test — undeclared dep fails tsc, restricted import fails eslint)*

- Workspace contains `apps/api`, `apps/web`, `apps/portfolio`, `packages/{core,db,llm,scoring,config}` with `packages/config` providing shared tsconfig + eslint consumed by all.
- `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm test` run from the root across all workspaces and pass (empty tests OK).
- Root README documents layout and commands.

### M0-03 · Local infrastructure & env validation · **S** · `done` *(2026-07-13: Postgres 16 healthy via compose; persistence verified — probe table survived `down`/`up` on the `pgdata` named volume; boot fails fast naming the bad variable (verified for missing `DATABASE_URL` and non-numeric `API_PORT`); env schema in `apps/api/src/env.ts` is the single source of truth, with a test asserting every schema key is documented in `.env.example`)*

- `docker compose up -d` starts Postgres 16 with a persistent volume.
- `.env.example` documents every variable; env is zod-validated at API boot; boot fails fast with a clear message on any missing/invalid variable.

### M0-04 · Fastify skeleton with layering · **M** · `done` *(2026-07-13: `/health` serves `{ status, version }` with version from package.json; pino JSON logs carry a UUID reqId (or the caller's `x-request-id`); error-body contract ratified as canonical (2026-07-13) and verified live in both modes — stacks never appear in any response body; dev 500s may expose `error.message`, production 500s are fully generic, intentional 4xx messages pass through in both modes; example slice `GET /example/items[/:id]` is the layering reference — stays in-memory by design (swap retired at M0-06: no example table in the nine-table schema v1; real repositories live in packages/db); 8 integration tests via `fastify.inject`)*

- `GET /health` returns `{ status, version }`; pino structured logs with request IDs; centralized error handler returns `{ error: { code, message } }` and never leaks stack traces in production mode.
- Example route → service → repository slice exists as the layering reference (no SQL outside `packages/db`).
- Integration test hits `/health` through a real Fastify instance.

### M0-05 · CI pipeline · **S** · `done` *(2026-07-13: `ci` workflow runs typecheck/lint/test as matrix jobs on every push and PR to master — Node from `.nvmrc`, pnpm via `corepack enable` from the `packageManager` pin (pnpm/action-setup rejected: both its self-installer and standalone paths broke on the runner), pnpm store cached, concurrency group cancels superseded runs; verified green in run 29286254779 alongside the existing gitleaks + profile-guard jobs; checkout bumped to v7 across workflows (parked as "v5" in M0-03, but v7 was current by execution time); dependency-cruiser DEFERRED with adoption criteria in README (parked from M0-02); badges in README; ruleset `master-protection` requires all five checks on PRs with repository-admin bypass, so solo direct pushes still land — "red check blocks merge" holds for any non-bypassed merge)*

- GitHub Actions runs typecheck + lint + test + gitleaks on every PR and push to main; a red check blocks merge; badge in README.

ADDENDUM (2026-07-17, default-branch rename): found during the M1-06 plan-gate external review — the repository's default branch was `master` while this story's own acceptance bullet (above) and parts of the protocol said `main`. Resolution (Carlos, 2026-07-17): the `master-protection` ruleset was re-targeted from the named branch to Default-branch BEFORE the rename (zero unprotected window; Settings verified post-rename: Target = Default branch, Enforcement Active, bypass list EMPTY, five required checks present), then the default branch was renamed master → main (head unchanged at 7fcb818; master returns 404; nothing landed directly on main). Enforcement re-proven by a direct-push probe — empty commit 5d6ae48, declined, never left his machine, local reset to 7fcb818 — captured verbatim: `remote: error: GH013: Repository rule violations found for refs/heads/main.` / `remote: - 5 of 5 required status checks are expected.` / `remote: - Changes must be made through a pull request.` / `! [remote rejected] main -> main (push declined due to repository rule violations)` — both the require-PR rule and the 5-of-5-checks rule fired on refs/heads/main. Follow-ups landed in this change: ci.yml push trigger + ruleset comments (re-worded to reference the ruleset by role, "the default-branch ruleset", so they stay true under any future ruleset rename), security.yml branch filter, privacy-check.mjs base-branch fallback (with scratch-repo probe: fallback resolution + planted-fictional-token detection, per the gate-modification law), and the two CLAUDE.md workflow lines. Historical ledger text mentioning master (M0-05, M0-08, M1-02, M1-04, M1-05) is deliberately unrewritten — the record stands, the event is appended.

### M0-06 · Drizzle setup + schema v1 · **M** · `done` *(2026-07-13: nine tables exactly per story scope, plain SQL migration `0000_luxuriant_beast.sql` checked in and human-reviewed (gate V2 walkthrough, approved as applied baseline); enum-like columns are text + CHECK derived from `packages/core` as-const value sets (pg enums rejected — ALTER TYPE vs forward-only ADR-0003; TS enums banned under type stripping); ratified decisions: `sessions` shape invented (uuid id, unique token_hash, expires_at — not in original ERD), user_id added to applications/application_events per ADR-0007, repository interfaces co-located with Drizzle impls in packages/db ($inferSelect row types), Postgres-down = fail-fast with colima/compose hint, no skip; users+sessions repositories with integration tests against dockerized Postgres — global setup derives careerforge_test from DATABASE_URL, creates it, migrates; TRUNCATE-between-tests isolation with serial test files (suite slowness = the ADR-0004-style trigger to revisit); `pnpm db:seed` = idempotent fictional Alex Rivera profile only; CI test job split from the matrix with a postgres:16 service, check name `test` unchanged for the ruleset. Dispositions — PARKED: add indexes on FK columns (plain user_id FKs, application_events.application_id — UNIQUEs are already indexed) when EXPLAIN shows sequential scans on a real query path; earliest plausible M1-09/M1-10. PARKED: natural-key uniqueness on profile tables for M0-08's idempotent import. DOCUMENTED: esbuild build scripts denied in pnpm-workspace.yaml allowBuilds (postinstall is a perf shim; drizzle-kit verified working without it). DISMISSED: drizzle-kit's deprecated @esbuild-kit subdeps (upstream, warning-only). RETIRED: M0-04's example-slice swap promise — the slice stays in-memory as the layering reference.)*

- Drizzle + drizzle-kit configured; `pnpm db:migrate` applies checked-in SQL migrations; migration files reviewed in PR.
- Schema v1: `users`, `sessions`, `profile_skills`, `profile_experiences`, `profile_projects`, `search_criteria`, `job_postings`, `applications`, `application_events` (per ARCHITECTURE.md ERD).
- Repository integration tests run against dockerized Postgres.

### M0-07 · Session auth · **M** · `done` *(2026-07-13: hand-rolled per ADR-0007 — 256-bit CSPRNG token (base64url) in a `cf_session` HttpOnly/SameSite=Lax/Path=/ cookie, Secure iff production, SHA-256 in `sessions.token_hash`; NO signing secret (ratified: HMAC adds nothing to a DB-verified random capability — ADR-0007 amended to Accepted, RISKS S-03 inventory now LLM key + `AUTH_BOOTSTRAP_PASSWORD`; invariant recorded: GETs never mutate, or the Lax CSRF posture breaks); argon2id via @node-rs/argon2 (no postinstall — pnpm build-script denial holds; OWASP m=19456/t=2/p=1 as named constants; ambient-const-enum workaround pinned by a `$argon2id$` unit test; CI test job green on linux-x64-gnu IS the evidence its prebuilt binary loads there); 7-day absolute TTL, rotation = insert-new+delete-old, cleanup = lazy delete on expired presentation + `deleteExpired` sweep on login (no timers); guard = root onRequest hook, opt-OUT via `config.public` (allowlist exactly GET /health + POST /auth/login, asserted by an onRoute-collector test through a buildApp seam), is404 passes through to keep the 404 contract; CSRF = Lax + Origin check on mutations incl. login (allowlist = `WEB_APP_ORIGIN` only); rate limit = hand-rolled per-IP fixed window 10/15min with injectable clock (deterministic tests, zero timers); enumeration defense = identical 401 body + boot-time dummy-hash verify on unknown email (asserted structurally, no wall-clock tests); bootstrap user from required `AUTH_BOOTSTRAP_EMAIL/PASSWORD` at first boot in main.ts only, idempotent, boot log states env password changes do NOT update it, password-never-logged asserted by capture; apps/api gained @careerforge/db and integration tests on the shared harness (packages/db exports ./test-utils + ./test-setup; vitest `groupOrder` stages db→api so cross-project TRUNCATEs can't race); example slice now guarded (authenticated coverage moved next to the auth tests). Dispositions — DISMISSED: in-memory rate limiter resets on API restart (acceptable at single-user localhost scale, ratified 2026-07-13). RATIFIED: seed's fake password hash is permanently unverifiable — the example user can never authenticate; verifyPassword returns false, never throws, on malformed hashes. PARKED: password rotation/change flow (edit env + delete user row is the manual path) — named future story if ever needed. OUT (unchanged scope): registration, password reset, /auth UI (M0-10), OpenAPI schemas (M0-09), CORS wiring (M0-10 — `WEB_APP_ORIGIN` already landed).)*

- Single user seeded from env at first boot (argon2id hash). `POST /auth/login` sets an HTTP-only `SameSite=Lax` signed cookie backed by a `sessions` row; `POST /auth/logout` revokes it; `GET /auth/me` returns the user.
- All non-auth/non-health routes 401 without a valid session; login is rate-limited; session rotates on login and expires.
- Tests cover: wrong password, expired session, rotation, rate limiting.

### M0-08 · Profile importer · **M** · `done` *(2026-07-13: first code to touch real career data — P-01 guardrails were explicit requirements. Source format grew two pieces (the schema's NOT NULL `level` and `provenance` had no markdown home; never inferred, never fabricated): NEW `skills.md` table (`| Skill | Category | Level | Years | Last used |` — resume.md's Technical Skills prose stays human-facing, unparsed) and a required `**Provenance:**` line per projects.md entry, both demonstrated in docs/profile.example/. Parser is pure (apps/api/src/modules/profile), reads resume.md's `## Professional Experience`; every deviation = ParseIssue{file, line}, all issues aggregated into one 422/CLI report, nothing silently skipped and nothing imported until clean (malformed fictional fixture pins exact file+line triples); date convention (documented, not fabrication): "March 2020" → 2020-03-01, year-only → Jan 1 / Dec 31 interval bounds, "Present" → NULL. M0-06's PARKED natural-key uniqueness came due → RESOLVED by forward-only migration 0001 (design approved pre-write 2026-07-13): UNIQUE indexes (user_id, lower(name)) on profile_skills/profile_projects (case-insensitive) and (user_id, company, title, start_date) on profile_experiences (boomerang rehire stays representable). Sync semantics (approved): full mirror in one transaction — app-level select-then-upsert on the natural keys (DB indexes are the backstop, asserted by a 23505 test), unchanged rows never rewritten, rows absent from markdown deleted; so identical re-import reports all-zero counts, THE idempotency evidence (gate run: cold example import +8/+3/+3 skills/experiences/projects with projects linked to experiences by company — latest stint wins — then re-import all zeros; seed.ts realigned to exact parser output so seed→import is also a no-op). Professional project naming a company with no resume experience = hard parse error (approved link policy). User resolution: POST /profile/import imports into the session user (guard-protected, no user id in the request); CLI resolves AUTH_BOOTSTRAP_EMAIL, or the seed user with --example. Privacy hard rules held: tests/fixtures parse docs/profile.example/ + a fictional malformed fixture ONLY; buildApp's profileDir under NODE_ENV=test defaults to a nonexistent sentinel so a test that forgets to inject fails loudly instead of reaching docs/profile/ (asserted); parse-issue values (which quote profile content) go to CLI stderr ONLY — pino gets issue counts + filenames, never values (AMENDED 2026-07-14, privacy-gate review: HTTP 422 bodies originally carried the quoting messages too; they now carry a redacted projection — file/line/field/rule — so the API never echoes profile content); privacy gate evidence: 142 distinctive real-profile tokens (emails, URLs, headings, bold spans, table cells from docs/profile/*.md) grepped against the full PR diff by a masked-output script = zero hits, and docs/profile/ confirmed gitignored with zero tracked files. Real-profile smoke: run manually by Carlos (never automated) — requires authoring docs/profile/skills.md + Provenance lines first. Dispositions — RESOLVED: M0-06 parked natural-key uniqueness (migration 0001). PARKED: job-criteria.md → search_criteria import (M1-08 formalizes the jsonb shapes; schema comment already points there). RATIFIED: skills.md is the machine-readable skills source; resume prose is display-only. RATIFIED: markdown is the single source of truth — full-sync deletes, no stale rows. OUT (unchanged scope): profile CRUD routes (GET/PUT /profile etc., M0-09+), web UI rendering (M0-10). RETRO 2026-07-14: the external second-opinion review of this story ran to completion — every item implemented, verified, or parked with a named owner; two of its concerns produced durable artifacts (scripts/privacy-check.mjs in the repo; the declared/earned/effective level-ownership park on M3-06). FINDING → RESOLVED (2026-07-14→15): shipping this retro as a direct push to master (1ad8be6) revealed branch protection still permitted direct pushes — the no-bypass guarantee was protocol, not enforcement; the push was benign (reviewed tree, green CI) but exercised the gap. Closing took two steps, because the first verification attempt FALSIFIED the fix: require-PR was added to the master-protection ruleset (2026-07-14), but the enforcement test — an attempted direct push (c563de0) — BYPASSED it via the ruleset's inherited M0-05 bypass actor (RepositoryRole admin, mode always), which covers every rule in the ruleset; that push also shipped a disposition claiming "verified by rejection" before any rejection existed (corrected here). Bypass actor removed 2026-07-15 — bypass list now EMPTY, deliberately not a PR-only bypass mode (that would still allow merging red checks); the emergency hatch is editing the ruleset itself, deliberate friction and audit-logged. Second direct-push attempt REJECTED (GH013 "push declined due to repository rule violations — Changes must be made through a pull request", exit 1); this correction reached master via the first PR to land under full enforcement. LESSON (general): outcome-describing text is authored AFTER the outcome exists, never from the expected result — the false claim shipped with a green gate because the gate cannot check claims about tests that have not run; evidence-before-claims extends to verification narratives.)*

- `POST /profile/import` (and a CLI script) parses `docs/profile/` markdown into profile tables; running against `docs/profile.example/` seeds the demo profile; import is idempotent (re-import updates, doesn't duplicate).
- Skills carry `level` (expert/solid/rusty/learning), `years`, `last_used`; projects carry `provenance` (professional / personal / personal_ai_assisted).
- Parse failures report file + line, never silently skip. Tests use the example profile only.

### M0-08b · Bootstrap password sync + direct-node CLI smoke guard · **S** · `done` *(2026-07-14: M0-07's PARKED password-rotation story came due (real AUTH_BOOTSTRAP_PASSWORD rotated; old value had `$` chars that shell/Compose interpolation mangled) → RESOLVED by `pnpm auth:sync-bootstrap`: reads AUTH_BOOTSTRAP_EMAIL/PASSWORD from the zod-validated env (parseEnv — its min-12 also catches interpolation-mangled values; CLI #4 is the first CLI to use parseEnv rather than ad-hoc checks), verifies the stored hash first (match = stated no-op, nothing written, sessions survive — idempotency evidence), otherwise re-hashes with the pinned argon2id constants and calls the new `UsersRepository.rotatePasswordHash` — UPDATE users.password_hash + DELETE all the user's sessions in ONE transaction (a rotated credential must invalidate live capabilities; single repo method spanning two tables per the syncProfile precedent). RETIRED: M0-07's "edit env + delete user row" manual path — post-M0-08 every profile table cascades from users (migration 0000 §FKs), so deleting the row would destroy the imported profile; the boot log and .env.example now point at auth:sync-bootstrap instead. Secrecy invariants: password read from env only (never argv), never printed on any path — sync-bootstrap.test.ts asserts no-password-values on all three result paths (rotated / already-synced / user-missing) with fixture creds; CLI output = status + counts + user id only; parseEnv errors name variables, never values. Direct-node smoke guard CREATED (M0-08 close-out follow-through, design ratified 2026-07-14): cli-smoke.test.ts runs all 4 CLIs (db:migrate, db:seed, profile:import, auth:sync-bootstrap) under `node <cli>.ts` with an empty env, asserting exit 1 + the missing-variable message + no SyntaxError + empty stdout; valid because every CLI statically imports its full module graph at top level, so ES-module linking trips strip-only parse errors before the env check exits — verified per CLI: migrate.ts/seed.ts/import-profile.ts/sync-bootstrap-password.ts all static, zero dynamic imports in packages/db, apps/api, packages/core non-test source; no force-loading needed. Node floor for `--env-file-if-exists` (≥22.9) satisfied: .nvmrc=24, engines >=24, CI reads .nvmrc — same wiring as the existing 3 CLIs. No schema change, no migration, no new ADR (operational extension of ADR-0007). Manual verification by Carlos: new password 200, old password 401, pre-rotation cookie 401. Dispositions — PARKED: a dynamic import() introduced anywhere in a CLI module graph would silently shrink the smoke guard's coverage (module linking would no longer load that subtree before the env check, and the guard stays green); candidate fix is a lint rule banning dynamic imports in CLI graphs — decision deferred. PARKED (schema review, 2026-07-14): (1) `profile_projects.provenance` conflates two dimensions — origin (professional/personal) and AI-assistance — in one CHECK-constrained column; split before any consumer needs to filter on one dimension independently. (2) Hard-erroring a professional project whose company has no resume.md experience assumes resume.md is complete and company names never diverge (renames, acquisitions); re-examine when profile CRUD (M0-09+) allows direct edits. (3) Whitespace normalization is APP-SIDE ONLY — a " TypeScript" probe proved the lower(name) unique indexes accept whitespace variants the parser would trim; every future writer MUST route through the parser's shared normalization, and whether to also enforce in-DB (trigger vs generated column) is an open question. (4) Dump/backup gitignore reasoning lives in .gitignore itself: no dump naming variant may ever be commit-eligible; /*.sql is root-scoped so committed migrations stay unaffected. RECORDED (privacy surface, approved design): local Claude session transcripts and scratchpads contain MASKED real-profile tokens — first 2 characters + length, emitted by scripts/privacy-check.mjs — so transcript/scratchpad hygiene is a known surface; these files are local-only and must never be published. ADDENDUM (2026-07-15, M1-03 kickoff): transcripts can also carry accidental UNMASKED values via editor-selection context attachment — an .env line rode into a session as selected text (AUTH_BOOTSTRAP_PASSWORD; disposition ACTED-ON, rotated same day) — not only masked tokens by design; the rotation-by-default rule and the .env-closed-during-agent-sessions habit are now CLAUDE.md hard-rule law.)*

- `pnpm auth:sync-bootstrap` applies a changed env password to the existing bootstrap user: re-hash in place + revoke all sessions, one transaction; running it again is a stated no-op.
- The password value is never accepted as an argument, never logged, never printed on any path; errors name variables only.
- All four CLIs are covered by an automated direct-node smoke guard (empty env → clean variable-naming failure).

### M0-09 · OpenAPI docs · **S** · `done` *(2026-07-14: fastify-type-provider-zod v7 wired into buildApp (validator + serializer compilers) and every route — health/auth/example/profile — now declares zod params/body/response schemas; ADR-0002 flipped Proposed → Accepted (this was its stated promise). The hand-rolled login safeParse + InvalidLoginBodyError DELETED: body validation runs pre-handler, and the never-echo property became ARCHITECTURAL — the error handler's VALIDATION_ERROR branch builds messages from validationContext + instancePath + zod issue CODE only, never issue.message (enum/literal messages quote received values; an M1 posting-status enum would have silently started echoing) — pinned by an enum-mismatch probe asserting the submitted value is absent, plus the login-specific test. Spec decision (delegated, disclosed): COMMITTED at docs/api/openapi.json (OpenAPI 3.1.0, 7 paths) + drift-as-a-test — a committed spec makes every API-surface change diff-visible in review, and openapi-drift.test.ts regenerates in-memory and byte-compares inside `pnpm test`, so drift fails the local gate AND CI's required `test` check with zero workflow/ruleset changes. Generation: `pnpm openapi:generate` → generate-openapi.ts (5th CLI) renders via the shared openapi.ts path from a fixed inert SPEC_ENV — the spec is env-independent by construction, so the CLI reads no process.env at all (deterministic, CI-safe; pg.Pool lazy, nothing connects). /docs UI: @fastify/swagger-ui at routePrefix /docs registered iff NODE_ENV !== 'production' (apps/api/src/routes/docs.ts); its auth exemption is a scoped onRoute hook marking config.public (swagger-ui has no per-route config passthrough) — the same deliberate opt-out as /health; @fastify/swagger itself registers in every env (route-less in-memory builder only). Guard-the-guard: allowlist test now pins the exact dev public set (health, login, + 6 swagger-ui routes) AND the production set (exactly GET /health + POST /auth/login — no /docs, no exemption). cli-smoke guard gained a second assertion mode: env-free CLIs assert exit 0 + expected stdout shape (openapi:generate has the largest module graph of any CLI — the strongest strip-only tripwire), env-required CLIs keep the exit-1 variable-naming assertion. FINDINGS (both implementer-found) → RESOLVED: (1) a green test proven blind by probe, then fixed — the AppDeps.onRoute collector snapshotted config.public BEFORE scoped onRoute hooks ran, so the allowlist test kept passing its two-route pin while /docs actually served unauthenticated; a probe printed the collected flags against live behavior to prove the blindness before the fix, and the seam now exposes `public` as a live getter over the final route config, read after ready(). (2) second-order review of the accepted smoke-guard amendment: smoking generate-openapi as specified would have regenerated the committed spec on disk before the drift test read it, permanently masking exactly the drift it exists to catch → --out <path> flag; the smoke writes to a tempdir. (3) found by this story's own gate run, in two acts — first live FAIL of the privacy gate was a false positive: a public lockfile dependency name collided with a real skills.md cell and was flagged from a diff CONTEXT line (the branch added no occurrence; the string already exists in master's lockfile, i.e. already public); scripts/privacy-check.mjs now scans ADDED lines only — deleted/context lines are base-branch content by definition — proven by scratch-repo probe before adopting: an added real-profile token still exits 1, the same colliding token in an ADDED line still exits 1, a context-only occurrence exits 0 (no detection lost for anything a branch introduces; a real token in a context line would be a pre-existing incident outside a per-branch gate's scope). Second act: the re-run FAILED again because the first draft of this very disposition (and the fix's code comment and commit message) named the token while stating it matched a real skills.md cell — documentation of the false positive was itself a real leak, caught by the fixed gate scanning added lines; all three texts were reworded to carry no token value (masked form exists in the local session record only, a surface already RECORDED in M0-08b). Sizing honesty: sized S, executed as M — the kickoff premise "type provider is already the route pattern" was falsified at plan time (auth.routes.ts said the wiring "lands with M0-09"); plan mode caught the false premise before execution, ask-first working upstream. Rode along: the ledger law (outcome-describing text is authored AFTER the outcome exists) codified in CLAUDE.md hard rules. Gate evidence 2026-07-14: typecheck + lint + test all green (117/117, incl. drift test against the committed spec); manual dev server: GET /docs 200 (HTML UI), /docs/json = the 3.1.0 spec, unauthenticated /example/items still 401; NODE_ENV=production boot: /docs and /docs/json 404, /health 200. Spec truthfulness notes: the committed spec is generated from a dev-mode build but IS the production API surface by construction — swagger-ui marks its routes `schema: { hide: true }` and @fastify/swagger excludes hidden routes, and /docs is the only env-dependent surface (documented in openapi.ts + ARCHITECTURE §5). The 403 responses on POST /auth/login, /auth/logout, /profile/import were not in the plan — the spec documented reality the plan omitted: the root guard's CSRF origin check (auth.hooks.ts, ForbiddenOriginError) runs on mutating methods only, which is also why GETs carry no 403 (Lax posture + GETs-never-mutate invariant, ADR-0007). Dispositions — RATIFIED: 422 responses enumerate PARSE_RULES (now a runtime const) in the spec; the serializer enforces the redacted-issue projection on the wire (defense-in-depth: undeclared fields are stripped). RATIFIED: the safe-integer minimum/maximum bounds zod v4 emits on int fields are accepted serialization noise — never hand-suppress; removal would trip drift for zero gain. PARKED: OpenAPI tags/operation summaries and shared $ref components — polish, revisit when the surface grows past one screen (M1). PARKED (appended 2026-07-15, M0-10 kickoff): the committed-spec-equals-production-surface claim rests on /docs being the only env-dependent surface (`schema.hide`) — unpinned by any test; when M1 grows the surface, revisit whether the drift test should build both envs and diff them. OUT (unchanged scope): profile CRUD routes (M0-10+), typed web client generation from the spec (M0-10 uses packages/core types).)*

- Route zod schemas generate an OpenAPI spec served at `/docs` in dev; spec is committed or CI-verified so drift fails the build.

### M0-10 · Nuxt web shell · **M** · `done` *(2026-07-15: two PRs, because BOTH kickoff premises about existing infrastructure were falsified at plan time — no profile read path existed (only POST /profile/import; CRUD was parked "M0-10+") and no CORS was wired (M0-07's explicit park) — stop-first honored on both API touches before planning finished. DISPOSITION (dictated at kickoff resolution): second consecutive story where an acceptance criterion presumed infrastructure that didn't exist (M0-09 type provider, M0-10 profile read path + CORS) — BACKLOG was authored before dependency order was real; M1 plan modes should expect the same skew and verify premises against code first. SERVER PR (#7, merged c4a1446): GET /profile = flat arrays (skills/experiences/projects, nullable experienceId), DB truth, no view shaping; wire contract profileResponseSchema lives in packages/core; repository-only reads with deterministic ordering (skills category+lower(name), experiences start_date desc + lower(company)/lower(title) tiebreaks, projects lower(name)); serializer strips undeclared row fields — pinned by exact toEqual (a stray user_id/timestamp fails the test). CORS: @fastify/cors registered BEFORE the guard — hook order IS the preflight auth exemption (preflights never carry cookies), pinned behaviorally beside the allowlist tests; origin = one-element ARRAY of the exact validated WEB_APP_ORIGIN (FINDING, caught by the new pin failing against the obvious form: a bare-string origin is emitted UNCONDITIONALLY with no comparison — the array form compares exactly, so an unlisted origin gets NO allow-origin header); credentials true; @fastify/cors owns OPTIONS outright (non-preflight OPTIONS → its 400, never reaching router or guard — no new authed surface); foreign-origin mutations still 403 via the untouched CSRF check. The gate-modification law (codified in CLAUDE.md on this very branch) was applied on its first day: two planted misconfigurations — bare-string origin, cors-registered-after-guard — each proved the new pins FAIL (1/27 and 2/27), both reverted, suite then 27/27. Auth+error wire contracts (loginBody/sessionUser/loginResponse/errorEnvelope) moved from apps/api to packages/core with ZERO spec diff — the byte-identical committed spec is the behavior-neutrality evidence (drift test green throughout). WEB PR: apps/web = Nuxt 4, ssr:false (ADR-0001), zero Nitro server routes; global middleware mirrors the API's default-deny — session resolved ONCE via GET /auth/me (HttpOnly cookie: the server is the only truth), unauthenticated → /login?redirect=<target>, authed bounced off /login; typed client consumes packages/core types ONLY via type-only imports (RATIFIED: no runtime zod on trusted own-API responses — the API boundary validates; keeps zod out of the bundle); non-login 401 interceptor clears state → login with redirect; login page branches 401 (invalid credentials) / 429 (rate-limited). APPROVAL AMENDMENTS, both landed: (1) safeRedirect() — ?redirect= honors internal paths only; absolute URLs, protocol-relative //host, schemes, and non-strings (array query params) fall back to '/'; pinned by pure unit tests AND a mounted login-page test feeding an absolute URL. (2) vue/no-v-html = ERROR law in the shared eslint config (planted v-html on fictional data → lint FAILED exit 1 → reverted; gate law satisfied); profile view renders via interpolation only — escape discipline is architectural before M1-02's hostile posting text exists. apps/web got its eslint module wall: SQL/LLM SDKs + @careerforge/db|llm banned, @careerforge/core the sole internal dep. Component tests (the AC) run in the real nuxt vitest environment INSIDE the root runner — required @nuxt/test-utils + @nuxt/kit at the workspace root and explicit environmentOptions.nuxt.rootDir (the root runner's cwd holds no nuxt app; three startup failures diagnosed to get there). Tooling deviations, documented in the app README: tsconfig extends the GENERATED .nuxt/tsconfig.json + allowImportingTsExtensions (vue-tsc type-checks core's .ts-extension imports); apps/web excluded from type-aware ESLint (projectService can't see a generated tsconfig on fresh clones; nuxt typecheck/vue-tsc owns type safety); @parcel/watcher build script DENIED per the esbuild precedent (prebuilt optionalDeps, chokidar fallback); .nuxtrc (one generated test-utils registration line) committed so test runs don't dirty the tree. Gate 2026-07-15: typecheck + lint + test green, 137/137 (126 → +12 web, −1 deleted stub). Manual smoke per CLAUDE.md (scratch careerforge_smoke DB; throwaway bootstrap pair created ON the seed email so profile:import --example lands on a loginable user — everything fictional; artifacts deleted, DB dropped): curl matrix all-pass — login 200+allow-origin+cookie, GET /profile 200 with exact wire keys and 8/3/3 example rows, no-cookie 401, foreign-origin login 403, unauthenticated preflight 204, foreign preflight carries NO allow-origin header, logout 204 then old cookie 401. FINDING (smoke, evidence-before-claims working late): the first "SPA serves on :3000" check was FALSE evidence — an unrelated pre-existing node server on this machine holds :3000 and answered the curl; the web check was re-proven against markers of OUR app on --port 3005, and the collision is a real dev footgun (nuxt silently re-ports when :3000 is taken → browser origin no longer matches WEB_APP_ORIGIN → mutations 403) → warning box in the app README. FINDING (this branch's own gate run, the M0-09 pattern recurring): first live FAIL of the privacy gate since the added-lines narrowing — two real skills.md cells matched as SUBSTRINGS inside public npm package names in ADDED pnpm-lock.yaml lines (installing nuxt legitimately adds thousands of registry identifiers; the masked token forms live in the local session record only, per M0-08b's recorded surface) → RESOLVED: pnpm-lock.yaml excluded from the content scan — lockfile content is public-registry-derived by construction and private data cannot enter it through dependency resolution; gitleaks + tracked-file guard still scan it structurally; gate-modification law applied via scratch-repo probes (doc-line token exits 1 before AND after; lockfile-line token 1→0; a mixed branch with both still exits 1), then the real gate re-ran PASS with the lockfile hits gone and every other token still checked. FINDING (PR CI, post-ledger): the test job failed on the fresh runner — apps/web/tsconfig.json extends the GENERATED .nuxt/tsconfig.json, and esbuild hard-fails a broken extends when transforming test files; local greens were an artifact of earlier nuxt commands having written .nuxt/ (the vitest nuxt ENVIRONMENT builds into its own test dir and never writes it) → RESOLVED: reproduced locally by deleting .nuxt/ (byte-identical failure), fixed with a plain-JS globalSetup running the prepare step iff .nuxt/tsconfig.json is absent (plain JS on purpose — it must transform without the tsconfig it exists to create), re-proven 137/137 from a deleted-.nuxt state. PROCESS SLIP, disclosed: that fix's first push went out with lint RED and a shell-mangled commit message — `pnpm lint | tail` masked the exit code (pipeline status = tail's), and backticks in a double-quoted -m string were command-substituted; both caught immediately after push, corrected by amend + force-with-lease before any review; lesson recorded: never pipe a gate command when its exit code gates the next step, and commit messages take heredocs, not -m with backticks. Dispositions — PARKED: Pinia (one useState key suffices for the shell; revisit when M1 list/detail state pressure arrives). PARKED: Playwright e2e (component tests carry the AC; justify when flows span pages, likely M1-02) → RESOLVED (2026-07-15, M1-02: the XSS AC + cross-page flow justified it exactly as predicted; harness details in the M1-02 ledger). RATIFIED: client performs no runtime validation of own-API responses (type-only contracts). OUT (unchanged scope): profile CRUD/editing UI, registration/password-reset UI, posting screens (M1-02). P-01 held: no screenshots taken this story; all smoke data fictional; smoke artifacts deleted at teardown.)*

- `apps/web` (Nuxt 4, SPA mode) with login page, authenticated layout, profile view rendering imported data, and a typed API client (types from `packages/core`).
- Unauthenticated access redirects to login; component test covers the auth guard.

---

## M1 — Job Intelligence MVP (Weeks 3–6)

### M1-01 · Posting ingestion (paste-only) · **M** · `done` *(2026-07-15: first M1 story, and the first whose kickoff premises HELD when verified against code (the M0-09/M0-10 skew lesson, applied pre-plan): `job_postings` already carried every needed column plus the `(user_id, content_hash)` unique dedupe boundary — NO schema change, no migration. `POST /postings` (guarded mutation: 401 no-session, 403 foreign-origin via the untouched CSRF check): wire contract `postings.ts` in packages/core; repository dedupe is race-safe INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING (no read-then-write window; constraint pinned by a 23505 backstop test). Approved semantics — dedupe hash = sha256 hex of WHITESPACE-NORMALIZED text (collapse runs + trim; copy-mechanics variance like CRLF/padding dedupes; matches M1-06's future quote-matching normalization; stable cache keys for M1-05's content_hash × prompt_id cache) while `raw_text` is stored VERBATIM as pasted (byte-identical XSS-payload readback test); duplicate paste = 200 + `{ posting, duplicate: true }` returning the STORED record, create = 201 (re-pasting is a normal action, not a 409 error; the status pair + boolean is the notice). Size cap at BOTH layers with named roles: zod max 100k chars on rawText = the clear field-level 400 (`body/rawText: too_big`, value-free by M0-09's VALIDATION_ERROR architecture — probe asserts the pasted value is absent); Fastify's default 1 MiB bodyLimit = transport backstop, with a >1 MiB test proving the pre-parse 413 flows through OUR error envelope, not fastify's default shape. Untrusted-boundary pins where the boundary starts: response carries NO rawText/contentHash/userId (exact toEqual; serializer strips undeclared fields); logs carry rawTextLength only — the no-posting-text-in-logs test captures real pino output through a new `AppDeps.logStream` seam and was proven by a planted violation (rawText logged → test FAILED → reverted; gate-modification law honored for a new enforcement). Spec regen same commit (9 paths), drift green throughout. Gate 2026-07-15: typecheck + lint + test 160/160 (137 → +23). Manual smoke (scratch `careerforge_smoke` DB, throwaway bootstrap pair, artifacts deleted, DB dropped): login 200 w/ Origin :4300, foreign-origin 403, create 201 w/ metadata trimmed, CRLF-variant re-paste 200 duplicate:true SAME id w/ late metadata ignored, over-cap 400 with zero marker echoes, mega-body 413 in our envelope, no-cookie 401, API log grep = zero posting-text occurrences. RODE ALONG (established pattern, no standalone PR): (1) CLAUDE.md gate-commands-run-bare convention; (2) the privacy-gate matching-semantics park (see Parked section) — CORRECTION disclosed: kickoff dictation said "three narrowings to date" but the repo record shows two (added-lines-only M0-09, lockfile M0-10), so the park states two; (3) port relocation :3000/:3001 → :4300/:4301 (binventory owns the old neighborhood) with the M0-10 squatter footgun ENFORCED, not documented — Nuxt/listhen exposes no strict-port option (verified: get-port-please has only fallback ranges), so `dev:web` gained a preflight (apps/web/scripts/assert-port-free.mjs) that refuses to start when 4300 is taken; SPEC_ENV port values also moved and the committed spec did not change — the drift-green run is the SPEC_ENV-inertness evidence. Port evidence, both halves loud (amendment 2): occupied 4300 → `pnpm dev:web` exit 1 with the full explain-and-fix message (no silent re-port); free 4300 → our nuxt banner `Local: http://localhost:4300/` + `id="__nuxt"` markers served; occupied 4301 → API boot exit 1 with a full EADDRINUSE stack (nothing swallows it — `main.ts` awaits listen at top level). Dispositions — PARKED: no Unicode normalization (NFC/NFD) in the content hash; trigger: first false-duplicate or missed-duplicate involving visually-identical text. RATIFIED: dedupe is first-write-wins on metadata — a duplicate paste's metadata is discarded and the stored record returns untouched (metadata editing out of scope; ARCHITECTURE's PATCH is status-only, M1-02+); the response returning the stored record makes the discard client-detectable, and M1-02's UI decides whether to surface it. RATIFIED (delegated micro-decision): display-only metadata (company/title/sourceNote) is trimmed at the service boundary, empty-after-trim → NULL; rawText is the one field never touched. DISMISSED (examined, not unexamined): `max(100_000)` counts UTF-16 code units, not bytes/graphemes — acceptable for a cost-bound cap. DOCUMENTED: content-hash helpers live in apps/api (not packages/core — node:crypto stays out of the browser-consumed package); hoist when M1-05/06 need them. DOCUMENTED: eslint script-globals block extended to `apps/*/scripts/**` (+`console`) for the preflight. FINDING (post-ledger, this branch's own gate run — the third consecutive story where the privacy gate's live FAIL was a false positive): a heading token from the real job-criteria.md — a single common English word — matched, as a standalone word, ordinary prose in this very ledger (masked form in the local session record only, per the M0-08b recorded surface). The park committed HOURS EARLIER on this same branch had pre-decided the fix class, and its trigger fired → RESOLVED by distinctiveness-based matching: tokens already occurring in the base branch's committed content are public vocabulary and are subtracted from the probe set (a real token already sitting in base content would be a pre-existing incident outside a per-branch gate's scope — the added-lines-only argument, applied to the probe side; genuinely private strings can never be in the base tree, so no detection this gate exists for is lost). Gate-modification law honored by scratch-repo probes BEFORE and AFTER: a distinctive fictional token added by a branch exits 1 on both sides of the fix (detection kept), the already-public-vocabulary token goes 1→0 (false positive resolved), a branch adding both still exits 1; the real gate then re-ran PASS with every remaining token still checked. OUT (unchanged scope): list/detail UI + escaped rendering and status transitions (M1-02), GET /postings (M1-02), extraction (M1-05), any LLM touch.)*

- `POST /postings` accepts pasted text + optional metadata (company, title, source note); text is stored raw, content-hashed for dedupe (duplicate paste → existing record returned with notice), and size-capped with a clear error.
- **Posting text is treated as untrusted from this moment**: stored verbatim, always display-escaped downstream.

### M1-02 · Posting list & detail UI · **M** · `done` *(2026-07-15: server-first PR pair per the M0-10 precedent — PR #10 (api) + the web PR. SIZING HONESTY: sized M, executed L once the paste form and the M1-01-deferred duplicate-notice obligation were absorbed (the M0-09 precedent). PREMISE LEDGER: GET /postings, GET /postings/:id, PATCH /postings/:id verified ABSENT at plan time (built here); job_postings.status verified PRESENT since M0-06 — NO schema change; the AC omitted any create path → paste form scoped in at kickoff. FOURTH consecutive story with a premise correction at plan time — RATIFIED: premise verification is the standing first act of plan mode, and BACKLOG story boundaries are drafts until a plan confirms them against code. SERVER (PR #10): list = metadata only, newest-first deterministic; detail = THE one rawText response (byte-identical readback pinned with an XSS payload); PATCH status-only. Transition law: extracted/scored are PIPELINE-OWNED (M1-05/M1-09 artifacts) and unrepresentable in the PATCH contract (['new','archived'] enum → value-free 400, probe-pinned); archive from any status (idempotent), unarchive only from archived, violations 409 INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION; updates are conditional (WHERE status = read value, RETURNING) — no read-then-write window, concurrent transitions surface as the same 409. The rawText wire-path law enforced four ways: list repo method never selects it; exact-shape toEqual on list/PATCH; a SPEC TRIPWIRE asserting the rawText property occupies exactly two spec locations (ingest request, detail 200) so any future echo fails the suite; logStream pin extended to read/transition paths. uuid params (malformed id = value-free 400, not a PG cast 500); missing and foreign postings 404 identically (no existence leak). PARKED (owner M1-05): unarchive restores 'new' — provably lossless today (no pipeline writer exists); artifact-derived restore comes due with extraction. WEB (this PR): /postings list (archived rows dimmed), /postings/:id detail, /postings/new paste form; typed client methods via type-only core imports. RENDERING LAW: interpolation into a pre-wrap <pre> — newlines preserved by CSS, NEVER by \n→<br> (the road back to v-html; vue/no-v-html has been a lint error since M0-10). Paste form pins (kickoff, all component-test-pinned): (1) dumb pipe — rawText submitted exactly as it sits in the textarea, no client trim/normalization, UI path ≡ curl path; (2) the duplicate notice renders the SERVER's boolean and lands on the STORED posting (?duplicate route), first-write-wins surfaced per the M1-01 ledger assignment; (3) no client echo — after submit the app navigates to detail and renders the GET response, textarea contents never re-display as saved content; (4) errors display API messages as received, value-free, no client-added paste preview. XSS AC: component test (payload renders as a text node — zero child elements, byte-identical textContent, no execution side effect) AND Playwright e2e — the M0-10 Playwright park RESOLVED here as predicted: one chromium spec, login through the real form (fictional throwaway bootstrap pair via main.ts env bootstrap), payload pasted through the REAL form, then asserted in a real DOM: byte-identical textContent, zero child elements, zero dialogs, no execution marker, computed white-space === pre-wrap (newline preservation PROVEN, not assumed), list→detail flow, and re-paste → ?duplicate=true on the SAME posting id with the banner and the late metadata visibly discarded. E2E HARNESS: dedicated ports 4310/4311 (reuseExistingServer: false = the loud-fail); scratch careerforge_e2e recreated+migrated at API boot and DROPPED in global teardown (clean slate locally and in CI, per approval amendment); NEW packages/db CLI `e2e-db.ts` create|drop — SQL stays behind the db module wall (apps/web only shells out), joined the cli-smoke guard (env check deliberately precedes the command check so the arg-less empty-env run names DATABASE_URL). CI: e2e runs INSIDE the required `test` job — check name unchanged, zero ruleset edits; browsers cached by playwright version; RATIFIED WITH INSTRUMENTS (approval amendment): retries are CI-ONLY (retries: 2, trace-on-first-retry) while local stays 0 so flake is loud in dev, and the SPLIT TRIGGER is recorded — >~5 specs or >3 min added to the job graduates e2e to its own job/check. FINDING → RESOLVED: Playwright starts webServers BEFORE globalSetup — the first run died 3D000 on the missing e2e DB; DB creation moved into serve-api.mjs (the only place that provably runs before the API needs it), the drop stays in global teardown (which does run last). FINDING → RESOLVED: useAsyncData caches by key across mounts in the shared nuxt test app — two component tests failed on stale cross-test data; clearNuxtData() in beforeEach is now the pattern for page tests. FINDING → PARKED ("API graceful shutdown"): at suite shutdown the API child can dump a pg pool crash report on SIGTERM (severed connection mid-request; exit codes and results unaffected, suite green) — candidate fix is a SIGTERM handler in main.ts closing fastify then the pool; trigger: the first time the dump flips an exit code or muddies a CI failure. DEVIATION (disclosed) → PARKED (owner M4-03, re-homed at merge review): the e2e web server is `nuxt dev`, not the plan's preferred build+preview — NUXT_PUBLIC_* runtime injection into a prebuilt ssr:false payload is unverified, dev applies it deterministically. The open question (built-artifact behavior) belongs to the first real apps/web build, i.e. the platform-deployment decision, not to e2e suite growth — the split trigger was the wrong revisit hook. RATIFIED (merge review): the duplicate banner is query-param-triggered post-navigation — a hand-typed ?duplicate=true shows the informational banner (harmless static text), while the component test pins that only a server duplicate:true produces the navigation; this residue is accepted and must never be "fixed" into a client-side state-passing mechanism. DISMISSED (examined, kickoff amendment): browsers normalize textarea values to LF per the HTML spec, so a CRLF paste reaches the server as LF — platform-inherent, not client code (pin 1 governs code we write), dedupe-neutral via hash normalization, and curl keeps CRLF exercisable (PR #10's smoke proved byte-identical CRLF readback). RECORDED (kickoff): Carlos confirmed 2026-07-15 the real-.env migration to 4300/4301 and the two-terminal smoke — API 4301, web 4300, login + profile render, no 403s, binventory undisturbed on :3000 — closing the port relocation's final human-side verification. Gate evidence in the PR. FINDING → RESOLVED (post-merge, disclosed): the merge-review ledger touches RACED the merge — the commit was pushed to the PR branch, but the checks-watcher was armed before GitHub's rollup moved to the new head, so it exited green on the PRIOR commit's checks, and the merge API likewise merged the prior head; PR #11 landed WITHOUT the touches (caught by post-merge verification: a grep for the re-homed park returned 0 on master, then `git merge-base --is-ancestor` proved the commit absent). Recovery = this follow-up PR carrying the orphaned commit. RULE (general): after pushing to a PR branch, pin the head — `gh pr view --json headRefOid` must equal the pushed SHA — before trusting checks or merging; "checks green" is a claim about a specific commit, not about a PR.)*

- List with status (new/extracted/scored/archived), company, title, ingest date; detail renders raw text as escaped plain text (an XSS payload in a posting renders inert — test exists); status transitions available.

### M1-03 · Application tracking · **M** · `done` *(2026-07-15: server-first PR pair per precedent — PR #13 (api, MERGED f443853, ancestor-verified) + the web PR. PREMISE LEDGER: premises HELD, second consecutive story — `applications`/`application_events` shipped in M0-06 exactly per the ERD (posting_id UNIQUE enforced in the SQL, not just the diagram; stage/kind CHECKs match; applied_on/occurred_on present); NO schema change, no migration. OWNERSHIP CONTRAST with M1-02, inverted: ALL seven stages are user-driven (no pipeline writer exists or is planned) and the full stage enum sits in the PATCH contract; the append-only strictness lives in the EVENTS (M4-02 reads outcomes) — lenient graph, mandatory trail. Any DISTINCT stage reachable; every transition writes a system-authored stage_change event (detail `from → to`, closed-enum values) in the SAME transaction as the conditional update (WHERE stage = read value; stale view → 409, transaction writes NOTHING — the stale→zero-events atomicity pin lives at the repo layer). Same-stage PATCH = 409, a DELIBERATE divergence from M1-02's tolerated no-op: this PATCH has an event side effect, and rejecting no-ops preserves "every successful PATCH emits exactly one stage_change event". ONE WRITER at both layers: repo `insertEventRow` is the only application_events insert callsite; service `eventInsert` the only event constructor. `stage_change` is UNREPRESENTABLE in the events POST contract (['note','outcome'] — the M1-02 unrepresentability move applied to kinds; value-free 400, probe-pinned: response never contains the string). detail REQUIRED for both user kinds (a detail-free outcome carries nothing the terminal stage_change doesn't). Create mirrors M1-01: race-safe ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING, 200-duplicate/201-create, posting missing/foreign → identical 404. APPROVAL AMENDMENTS, both test-pinned: (1) response-schema completeness — every guarded route declares 401, every mutation 403; (2) appliedOn := the TRANSITION's occurredOn on first entry into 'applied' (defaulted to today only when absent, via the now seam; UTC-date default accepted, the web form always sends browser-local explicitly), NEVER server-now — backdated-transition test cites the amendment; first-entry-wins (re-entry never overwrites, trail keeps all three dates). DISMISSED (pre-empted by amendment 2): the planned "appliedOn correction" park — the wrong-date source was server-now, which no longer exists; a typo'd user date re-enters scope only with a real edit story. WEB: /applications list with SERVER-side stage filter (?stage=, stage-inclusive useAsyncData keys — the M1-02 caching finding applied at design time), detail with the trail (synthetic tracked-on head — no creation event by design: creation isn't a transition, created_at is the fact), stage control EXCLUDING the current stage, note/outcome form; every mutation re-fetches server truth. Create affordance on the posting detail ("created from a posting"): tracked-state probe = the ?postingId= list filter — the POSTING CONTRACT STAYS UNTOUCHED (zero ripple into M1-02's exact-shape pins); Track POSTs and navigates, created and duplicate landing on the same stored record; View link when tracked. RENDERING: event details are user-authored, not hostile — interpolation only anyway (the law is free); inert-payload component pin on the trail. NO rawText in any application payload — the M1-02 spec tripwire auto-covered the five new endpoints (stated, not re-proven; belt-and-braces exact-shape toEqual on the {company,title} summary). NO new Playwright spec (no new untrusted-rendering surface; split trigger untouched) but the suite re-ran green on the web PR because the XSS spec's page changed; the KNOWN parked pg-pool SIGTERM dump reproduced at teardown, exit codes unaffected — trigger unfired, park stands. EVIDENCE-BINDING, first live use of the promoted law: PR #13 head pinned (headRefOid == pushed SHA e384d9b) before checks were trusted, checks read from the SHA-scoped check-runs API (5/5 success ON the commit), merged by Carlos (per-PR merge word, carry-over 2), post-merge ancestor-verified (f443853). POST-MERGE REVIEW (Carlos, independent): ZERO findings — both amendments verified with their tests, all four carry-overs law, the rotation disposition bound to AUTH_BOOTSTRAP_PASSWORD by name, the atomicity pin confirmed at the repo layer. FINDING (session, exposure #2 — the new hard rule's first post-ratification instance): .env line 3 (POSTGRES_PASSWORD) rode into the session as an editor selection; rotation owed by default (local-only surface: ALTER USER + .env incl. DATABASE_URL), disposition owned by Carlos → ACTED-ON (2026-07-15, recorded at M1-04 kickoff): POSTGRES_PASSWORD rotated — ALTER USER + .env (incl. DATABASE_URL) updated. Gates: PR A 229/229 + scratch-DB smoke (create/duplicate/backdate/409/forged-400/trail/404/filters, detail canary absent from logs, throwaway pair, DB dropped, artifacts deleted); web PR 242/242 + e2e 1/1; privacy gate PASS on both branches after final commits. Manual verification by Carlos: PR #13 merged f443853; PR #14 (web) merged 3433c63, ancestor-verified. POST-MERGE REVIEW PR #14 (Carlos, independent, recorded at M1-04 kickoff): ZERO findings.)*

- Application created from a posting; stages: considering → applied → screen → interview → offer / rejected / withdrawn; events log stage changes, notes, and outcomes with dates; list view filterable by stage.

### M1-04 · `packages/llm`: provider interface + Anthropic adapter + prompt registry · **L** · `done` *(2026-07-15/16: single PR #15, branch m1-04-llm-provider, 9 commits + this ledger; SHA-scoped checks 5/5 on pinned head 9d07757 (headRefOid == pushed SHA) before anything was trusted. KEY HYGIENE FIRST, the recorded hard constraint honored in order: rotation runbook (docs/RUNBOOKS.md, create-before-revoke) written and plan-reviewed BEFORE the key existed; Carlos provisioned manually after runbook review — dedicated Console workspace, date-suffixed key. TIMELINE CORRECTION (amended in PR #16 pre-merge — the ledger records what happened, not what the runbook prescribed): the $20/mo hard cap + ~$10 alert were set 2026-07-16 AFTER the smoke and after the PR #15 merge, NOT at provisioning — a RUNBOOK-ADHERENCE DEVIATION (cap-last, inverting RUNBOOKS.md's set-at-provisioning prerequisite; Carlos's call, disclosed); spend while uncapped = the smoke only (~$0.002). LIVE SMOKE EVIDENCE (Carlos, 2026-07-16, pnpm llm:smoke): fixture-echo@v1 attempt 1 status=ok model=claude-sonnet-5 inputTokens=436 outputTokens=18 cacheReadTokens=0 latencyMs=3455, echo round-trip MATCH, key nowhere in output — adapter + registry + runner + recording proven against the real API exactly once, manually (ADR-0005 §4; pnpm test remains 100% mocked). Q1 ACTION ITEM: projection ~$3–8/mo at standard rates — flag condition NOT triggered (math in RISKS T-03). PREMISE LEDGER: extraction_runs ABSENT from schema v1 as suspected — fork resolved as the REQUIRED LlmCallSink seam (module boundaries force it: packages/llm cannot touch SQL; migration owed to M1-05 per the amended ERD — usage/latency/attempt columns, status vocabulary reconciled with the runner: ok|schema_failed|refusal|max_tokens|error set by the runner, flagged post-hoc by M1-06; refusal and max_tokens are NOT schema failures — truncation is a config bug distinguished via stop_reason, test-pinned even when the partial is invalid JSON). AC #3 (SDK quarantine) found ALREADY MET by the M0-02 eslint walls — scope became PROVING it (planted @anthropic-ai/sdk imports in packages/scoring AND apps/api → lint FAIL both, ARCHITECTURE §2 message). NEW premise conflict found at plan time: ADR-0005/T-03 "temperature 0" is unenforceable — claude-sonnet-5 rejects non-default sampling params (400) → adapter omits temperature unless configured; ADR-0005 AMENDED with official-docs citations, thinking-token variance named as the residual nondeterminism source (thinking: disabled as the control, exposed as GenerateRequest.thinking 'default'|'disabled' — the approval-refinement interface fix), structured-outputs subset lacks string-length caps → ADR-0006 layer-3 caps live in zod, intro-pricing expiry 2026-08-31 with the M2-retro re-check note. ADR-0005 STATUS: stays Proposed; flips Accepted at M1-05 when extract-then-score (its core rule) is realized — M1-04 realizes §§1–2. DESIGN NOW LAW: prompt versions are frozen DATA modules (no builder functions exist in the type shape — no interpolation site can reach a system prompt, ADR-0006 layer 1 structural, plus a prompts-directory lint rule banning interpolated template literals); stable name@vN ids with sha256 content pins — editing a shipped version fails the suite citing the versioning law, new behavior = new file + new pin; runPrompt is the single composition point (untrusted data → USER message only, FRESH random boundary token per wire call — the retry gets a new token, test-pinned); one retry on schema failure only, refusal/max_tokens never retry; EVERY call records a complete LlmCallRecord (prompt_id, provider, model, usage incl. cache counters, latency, raw response, status, attempt) to the sink — including thrown errors, value-free; raw responses never log (they can carry posting text). - `POST /postings/:id/extract` runs `extract-requirements@v1`: posting text passed as delimited data with a per-request random boundary token (ADR-0006 layer 1), single-turn, no tools, JSON-schema-constrained.
- Output zod-validated (one retry, then `schema_failed`); requirements stored with kind (must/nice), category, text, `source_quote`, confidence; an `extraction_run` row stores model, prompt_id, raw response, status.
- Re-extraction is explicit and append-only; cached by `content_hash × prompt_id`.
- **Named decisions for the M1-05 plan (recorded at M1-04):** (1) `extract-requirements@v1` — thinking disabled vs. default-with-low-effort; decide with cost + determinism rationale (ADR-0005 amendment 2026-07-15: thinking-token variance is the residual nondeterminism source now that sampling params are rejected). (2) Sink transactionality (external review F4): a throwing `LlmCallSink` currently converts a successful call into a thrown error — decide whether `runPrompt` guards sink failures or the sink contract is must-not-throw, alongside the persistence wiring.
- **Owed from M1-04:** the persistence sink — `extraction_runs` migration per the amended ERD (usage/latency/attempt columns; status vocabulary = runner's five states + post-hoc `flagged`), wired as the required `LlmCallSink` through the service layer. Parks riding along (external review F2/F3): deep-freeze for nested `jsonSchema` (Object.freeze is shallow) lands with `extract-requirements@v1`; `LlmCallRecord.timestamp` moves onto the now seam when the sink defines timestamp semantics.

The documented concurrent-double-POST residual observed live: append-only kept both runs, the conditional flip made the second a no-op (no status race), GET serves the latest ok run deterministically — the 13-req run, the one the quality read examined. Integrity: 15+13+15+29 = 72, all runs ok attempt 1, no orphaned rows; the first run's wire response was not captured client-side (abandoned curl) but its server persist line was recovered from scrollback (requirementCount 15) — all four runs have wire-or-log evidence matching their DB row counts (15/13/15/29). BONUS OBSERVATION: 15 vs 13 requirements for the same posting 10s apart — live confirmation of Decision 1's recorded residual (sampling variance remains; the determinism mechanism is the cache; ~±2-req variance data point for M1-13). Cost delta ≈ $0.02; leg-2 total ≈ $0.10, inside cap. FRICTION (M1-13): extraction is a silent 11–19s request and Ctrl-C aborts the curl, not the paid server-side call — M1-10's pending-state is the designed fix; until then fire once and wait (re-fire after completion is harmless: cache serves 200). ### M1-06 · Evidence verification · **M** · `done` *(2026-07-17: branch m1-06-evidence-verification off main@83c0947 — the first story on renamed main, behind the PR #19 rename follow-ups pre-task. - Every `source_quote` is whitespace-normalized string-matched against the stored posting; mismatches set `quote_verified = false` and mark the run `flagged`; flagged runs are visually prominent in the UI.
- Table-driven tests: exact match, whitespace variance, fabricated quote, near-miss paraphrase (must flag).

The gate is enforced from M1-07 forward: in a single PR the PR's class is the MAX of its slices, exactly one merge event exists, and a missing pre-merge glance is surfaced exactly like a failing check.

### M2-03 · CI quality gates · **M** · `done`

- Lighthouse CI budgets enforced on PRs (targets: performance ≥ 95, accessibility = 100, best-practices ≥ 95, SEO ≥ 95 on key pages); axe automated checks pass with zero violations; internal link check passes. A failing budget blocks merge.

> **Pre-task note (A2a, from M2-01):** verify against **live** Lighthouse docs — not memory — whether the best-practices category actually scores CSP (`Content-Security-Policy`). GitHub Pages cannot set response headers (ADR-0008); if the budget depends on header-based signals the host decision reopens (Cloudflare Pages is the named successor).
>
> **Pre-task note (from M2-01, 2026-07-20):** verify against **live** axe and Lighthouse docs — not memory — whether a duplicate `<h1>` is actually flagged by either gate, so the M2-02 fix (above) is validated by a real gate rather than an assumed one.

The three gates run in `portfolio-build` (already-required — no ruleset edit, empty bypass untouched) in order: `generate → assert-prerender → internal link check → [pin Playwright chromium + resolve CHROME_PATH] → lighthouse budgets → axe a11y`, all bare `- run:` (any non-zero fails the required check). Acceptance MET on the shipped `generate` build (H-2): perf/a11y/best-practices/SEO budgets on `/`, full axe zero-violations, internal link check — a failing gate blocks merge.*

*__PRE-TASK ANSWERS, RESOLVED BY OBSERVED RUN OUTPUT (not memory).__ A2a: EVERY response-header audit in Lighthouse best-practices is weight-0 (`csp-xss`/`has-hsts`/`origin-isolation`/`clickjacking-mitigation`) → ADR-0008's no-custom-headers limit touches only unweighted audits and does NOT reopen the host decision. A2b: a duplicate `<h1>` is flagged by NEITHER axe (`page-has-heading-one`/`heading-order` don't detect duplicates) NOR Lighthouse — but `assert-prerender.mjs`'s h1-count check covers it. R-2: best-practices applicable denominator MEASURED = 27 (not the ~31 the execution seat first estimated); ≥95 leaves ~a 2-audit cushion. R-3: `is-on-https` PASSES on `http://localhost:<ephemeral-port>` across 3 runs — the source-comment ambiguity is dead; CI audits the localhost artifact, not the HTTP-only prod origin. R-4: ship NO `robots.txt` and NO `sitemap.xml` — Lighthouse SEO scores robots.txt VALIDITY (not-applicable when absent) and has no sitemap audit; SEO = 100 with neither (PF-L2 confirmed: absent = N/A, invalid-present = fail). H-2 discharged; G7 discharged (ARCHITECTURE:29/:62/:383 now true and name all three gates); ADR-0009 minted, resolving `lighthouserc.cjs:1`'s formerly-dangling cite.*

*__WHAT THE GATES CAUGHT (pattern memory — none would have failed a unit test).__ (1) A PLANTED-FAIL THAT TESTED THE WRONG THING (axe): PF-A1 used `image-alt`, but `image-alt` IS a Lighthouse a11y audit (weight 10, verified in `default-config.js`) → it proved the runner bites, not that FULL axe adds coverage; corrected to PF-A2 `scrollable-region-focusable` (verified ABSENT from Lighthouse's 63-audit a11y list) with a blindness demo — axe exits 1 while the Lighthouse a11y=100 budget stays green. (2) A SECOND WRONG-TARGET PF (link check): the planned dead-anchor PF-5 is caught by `nuxt generate` ITSELF (Nitro prerender crawl → "Exiting due to prerender errors"), and a missing component asset fails Vite resolution — so a dead-anchor PF fails identically WITHOUT the gate; dropped, and link-check's UNIQUE coverage stated honestly = the asset-PATH class (PF-4 base-prefix, the M2-11 bug: `generate` succeeds, `assert-prerender` blind, link-check reds). (3) A SUPPLY-CHAIN BYPASS: `linkinator@8.0.0` was published the SAME DAY; `pnpm` auto-added a `minimumReleaseAgeExclude` bypass — reverted it and pinned the established `^7.6.1` instead of installing an hours-old release. (4) A MISDIAGNOSIS, self-corrected: an interim link-check header blamed linkinator's `serverRoot` for "mis-resolving" absolute asset paths; the real cause was INCOMPLETE builds (a dead anchor had failed `generate`, leaving `_nuxt` unwritten) — corrected in the same slice. (5) A PLANT RESIDUE: reverting the PF-6 axe plant via `git checkout -- <file>` restored it from HEAD (the committed plant), not the pre-plant baseline — the plant lingered in the tree; the `git diff --stat <baseline> HEAD` guard caught it before the glance, and the final head was restored from the baseline SHA and re-verified byte-identical. LAW reinforced: to revert a committed plant, `git checkout <baseline-sha> -- <file>`; and ALWAYS diff the final head against the green baseline before declaring green.*

*__CI-OBSERVED, SHA-SCOPED.__ `a2c739c` baseline wiring → green (every step ran, not vacuous). `ec37ecf` PF-6 → `portfolio-build` RED @ `axe a11y` (generate…lighthouse all success); other checks green. `b7492b6` PF-6b → RED @ `internal link check` in 32s, browser/lighthouse/axe SKIPPED (fail-fast, browser-free); other checks green. `4fc17d0` final → 6/6 green, tree byte-identical to `a2c739c`. Lighthouse's failure-propagation rests on the structural bare-`- run:` guarantee plus PF-6 showing it ran in the failure chain (no third slow CI round-trip).*

*__PARKS — DISCHARGED / CARRIED.__ DISCHARGED: R-4, G7, H-2, ADR-0009 mint + citation, F-2 boundary (into the ci.yml comment), the M2-11 "NOT GATED / OPEN until M2-03" comment (rewritten — now closed). **NO_FCP (OPEN-until-CI-observed):** a Lighthouse headless-paint `NO_FCP` was seen LOCALLY from accumulated Chrome on a long macOS session; it did NOT reproduce in CI (clean across all 3 lighthouse-executing runs). No speculative retry added — a retry on an ASSERTION failure would mask a real regression; if it ever fires, a follow-up adds a retry scoped ONLY to the `NO_FCP`/`ERRORED_DOCUMENT_REQUEST` runtime string. **Enforce HTTPS** — still OPEN-until-GitHub-issues-cert, operator-owned. **S3-2 (owner M2-05):** when a 2nd content page lands, extend the Lighthouse `assertMatrix` URL set + the axe/link crawl beyond `/`. **Series cost** on every PR (Chrome + 9 lighthouse runs + axe + linkinator) accepted (Option A); a paths-scoped skip is parked (would reintroduce a "green without running" surface). **Reproducibility:** the pinned Playwright chromium (not the runner's rolling system Chrome) keeps the budgets stable; a future `@playwright/test` bump moves the browser deliberately.*

*__WHY THE VALIDATOR CARRIES 100% OF ENFORCEMENT (source-verified, re-confirmed at runtime).__ `@nuxt/content` 3.15.0 validates NOTHING at ingest — its zod schema becomes JSON Schema for column typing only; the parse path never `safeParse`s; a missing required field inserts NULL, an out-of-enum value inserts verbatim. So `nuxt generate` builds GREEN on schema-violating content (PF-1 proved it: generate EXIT 0 with a bad-provenance-token file present, then the validator RED R2). The deterministic, browser-free, zero-dep node script is the whole enforcer.*

*__PLANTED-FAIL INDEX (rule-ids + counts only; every local plant reverted, tree diffed clean vs baseline `851dfcc`).__ PF-0 body-h1 → content-convention vitest RED (proves the shared-lib refactor didn't break the existing gate). PF-1 Content-v3 blindness → generate GREEN then validator RED R2. PF-2 missing section → R6. PF-3 out-of-order → R6. PF-4 empty Results → R7. PF-6 unresolvable citation → R8. PF-7 professional w/o attestation → R3. **PF-5 (live CI):** `content/case-studies/fictional-widget.md` with a digit-bearing Results claim and no citation → `portfolio-build` RED at step 6 `case-study content gate`, steps 7–15 (generate→lighthouse→axe) SKIPPED (run `29886368798`, job `88817664618`) — the gate blocks in CI BEFORE the job pays for build or Chrome; reverted via `git checkout 851dfcc -- <path>`-class (new file → `git rm`), re-green at `460d67a`. Coverage boundary honest: R4 duplicates content-convention ON PURPOSE (its unique legs = out-of-tree targets + reds inside `portfolio-build`).*

*__PRIVACY-GATE CATCH (pattern memory — the gate working).__ `scripts/privacy-check.mjs` went RED (6 leaks) on the slice-C fixtures BEFORE the first push. Localized (masked, values never printed): all six were `## N. Section` numbered headings reproduced verbatim from the private `docs/profile/case-studies-draft.md` (the draft lives INSIDE `docs/profile/`). The section taxonomy is public (this story's own bullet), but the numbered dot-form is distinctive to the private draft and absent from the base tree. FIX WITHOUT WEAKENING THE GATE: committed fixtures are UNNUMBERED (R6's numeric prefix is optional); numeric-prefix coverage preserved via a non-colliding `2. Problem` fixture + a runtime-assembled positive test (the literal `N. Section` never appears in committed source). Re-run: clean, EXIT 0. Also caught by CI's own `lint` job: an unused `scriptDir` + prettier reformats I'd missed by running typecheck-but-not-full-lint locally (fixed `460d67a`).*

*__LOCAL ACCEPTANCE RUN — real draft, scratch-only, uncommitted, deleted after (codes + rule-ids only, zero draft text). A pre-registered STOP fired and was ruled.__ Stage 1: EXIT 1, **4 R8 failures, all in §6 Results, R1–R7 CLEAN, no other rule** — the two malformed Results citations at PER-SOURCE granularity. ### M2-10 · Per-posting resume tailoring/export (Q5 promotion) · **M** · `done`

> Promoted from the Icebox at the M1 retro (Q5, operator decision 2026-07-19; dogfood evidence: prep value attested on all six in-flight applications, want stated unprompted). PROPOSED placement: after M2-09's publish, may run parallel to M3 at the operator's discretion — placement ratified at the M1-13 final review.

- From a reviewed fit report, generate a draft resume variant that reorders/emphasizes EXISTING verified profile content only — evidence links and gap classifications drive the emphasis, and every emphasis decision cites the requirement/evidence that motivated it. Never fabricates content, metrics, or experience (standing law; ADR-0006 lineage). Draft-until-reviewed; export (markdown first) is manual, never auto-sent. Generated variants embed real profile data: local-only artifacts, never committed (P-01).
- **PHASE 1 (this story).** Because experience bullets are (correctly) NOT in the DB, the output is a tailoring/emphasis **guide** over verified facts — a reordering with cited emphasis — **not** a submittable bulleted resume. The doc header, the UI copy, and this line all say so. Bullet-level tailoring is the additive phase-2 story **M2-12** (recorded in ADR-0012). **The DB holds no resume prose:** the LLM emits only a spec (ordering + emphasis over server-assigned refs), a deterministic renderer builds the markdown 100% from DB rows, so fabrication is impossible by construction (spec-not-prose; ADR-0012, new — not an ADR-0006 amendment).

*(2026-07-23 BUILD RECORD, one PR, eight reviewable commits — status `done` on code-complete + all gates green; the merge/SEAL line and the live Leg-2 example-profile demo are recorded at close, post-merge, per evidence-before-claims.)* Slices: (1) core enums + `resume.ts` wire schemas. (2) db schema + **migration 0008** (4 append-only tables: `resume_variant_runs`/`resume_variants`/`resume_variant_entries`/`resume_variant_citations`) + repository + dockerized-PG tests incl. the **snapshot pin** (re-import via `syncProfile` → entry FKs SET NULL, label/detail/rendered_markdown survive) + the both-route cascade trace + raw-SQL CHECK/UNIQUE probes; dev-migrate proof. (3) llm: `buildTailoringPayload` + `validateTailoringSpec` (membership + both-direction permutation) + **`resume-tailoring@v1`** (one new pin) — the reason is JUDGMENT-framed, never a satisfies-claim (N3). (4) core: deterministic `renderResumeVariantMarkdown` (`fenceFor` breakout-safe; every posting/LLM string fenced; experiences never reordered/omitted; provenance always present; template text em-dash-free — the exported variant is published-facing). (5) api: `resume` module (routes→service→repository) + wiring + OpenAPI regen; export bypasses the zod JSON serializer (identity serializer + `text/markdown` + uuid-only filename), 409s a draft, serves stored markdown byte-for-byte. (6) llm: tailoring adversarial corpus (4 fictional fixtures, third ADR-0006 layer-6 ingress) + evaluator + structural/source-byte tests + smoke CLI. ### M3-01 · Learning plans from gaps · **M** · `done`

- Select gaps (across postings) → `POST /learning-plans` drafts a plan citing, for each gap, the posting evidence that created it; recurring gaps across multiple postings rank higher; plans are draft-until-reviewed.

*(2026-07-24 BUILD RECORD, one PR, six reviewable slices — status `done` on code-complete + all gates green; live dogfood legs pending the review seat's go-ahead, see below. ### M3-03 · Mastery evidence · **M** · `done`

- Evidence records of kind **implemented / tested / explained / revisited** with artifact links (repo, test run, writeup) and dates; an exercise cannot be `complete` without at least implemented + tested evidence; a skill claim is only upgraded when evidence exists — passive reading counts for nothing (enforced in the model, not just the UI).
